# Verly — Features catalog (Q&A)

This document mirrors the structure of the public feature detail pages in `frontend-v2/app/features/[slug]/page.tsx`: hero and metadata, introduction, “what is it,” usage and workflow, how it works (function blocks), setup steps, value and use cases, and FAQ.

Each section uses **question-style headings** so you can scan or reuse copy for help center, SEO, or sales enablement.

**Quick index:** See [`FEATURES-LIST.md`](./FEATURES-LIST.md) for a compact list of every feature name by category.

**Images:** PNG placeholders live at [`docs/assets/features/feature-visual-placeholder.png`](./assets/features/feature-visual-placeholder.png). Swap in real screenshots per feature while keeping the same filenames or update the paths in the generator (`frontend-v2/scripts/export-features-catalog.ts`).

**Regenerate:** `cd frontend-v2 && npx --yes tsx scripts/export-features-catalog.ts`

**Source of truth:** [`frontend-v2/app/features/data.ts`](../frontend-v2/app/features/data.ts).

Inside Verly, ai answer improver fits naturally into the broader ai features for customer support experience. That means your team can use it without adding unnecessary operational overhead or disconnected tools.

### Q: What is this feature?

AI Answer Improver is part of Verly's ai features for customer support toolkit. It helps teams automate support with AI Chatbot and Answer Assistant so agents can move faster, customers get clearer outcomes, and operations stay easier to manage at scale.


**Definition highlights:**

- AI Answer Improver is designed to support teams working across ai features for customer support.
- It helps standardize execution so agents can move faster while customers receive a more predictable experience.
- The capability works best when paired with clear workflows, ownership, and the rest of your Verly setup.
- This page gives you a structured overview, usage guidance, and implementation ideas for ai answer improver.

- Combine it with your other Verly processes so agents can work with less friction and fewer manual gaps.
